### [57] [Secure Coding Drift in LLM-Assisted Post-Quantum Cryptography Development: A Gamified Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19474)
*R. D. N. Shakya,C. P. Wijesiriwardana,S. M. Vidanagamachchi,Nalin A. G. Arachchilage*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出后量子密码学（PQC）中的安全编码漂移概念，即长期依赖LLM生成代码会导致安全实践逐渐退化，并提出一种游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架作为解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向PQC过渡带来了巨大的实现复杂性，而LLM在软件开发（包括密码工程）中广泛使用，但常生成不安全或次优代码，尤其是在安全关键领域。需要解决长期依赖LLM生成代码导致安全实践逐渐退化的问题。

Method: 提出PQC安全编码漂移这一新颖的社会技术脆弱性模型，将安全风险概念化为源于人机交互的纵向行为现象。为缓解此问题，提出游戏化、LLM增强的安全编码框架，嵌入对抗性评估、行为反馈和安全评分。

Result: 提出了一种将LLM从被动助手转变为主动安全协作者的框架，有助于在AI中介环境中实现更安全的PQC实现。

Conclusion: 通过游戏化框架将LLM重塑为主动安全协作者，为AI辅助密码工程中的安全实践退化问题提供了创新解决方案。

Abstract: The transition to Post Quantum Cryptography (PQC) introduces considerable implementation complexity, requiring strict adherence to constant-time execution, side channel resistance, and precise parametrisation. Simultaneously, large language models (LLMs) are heavily embedded in software development workflows, including cryptographic engineering. While LLMs improve productivity, evidence shows that they frequently generate insecure or suboptimal code, particularly in security critical domains. This paper introduces Secure Coding Drift in PQC, a novel socio technical vulnerability model capturing the gradual degradation of secure coding practices due to sustained reliance on LLM-generated code. Unlike prior work that focuses on static vulnerabilities, we conceptualise security risk as a longitudinal behavioural phenomenon rising from human AI interaction. To mitigate this, we propose a gamified, LLM augmented secure coding framework that embeds adversarial evaluation, behavioural feedback, and security scoring into development workflows. Our approach reframes LLMs from passive assistants into active security co-pilots, contributing toward safer PQC implementation in AI mediated environments.

</details>


### [58] [FloatDoor: Platform-Triggered Backdoors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19535)
*Nils Loose,Jonas Sander,Felix Mächtle,Thomas Eisenbarth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 FloatDoor，首个输入无关、平台触发的后门攻击方法，利用 LLM 在不同部署平台上的数值差异（由非结合浮点运算和不同内核实现导致），通过两个轻量 LoRA 适配器实现攻击：一个放大平台间数值分歧，另一个将平台签名绑定到恶意下游任务。攻击在目标平台触发恶意行为，在其他平台保持良性，利用了模型审计与部署之间的 TOCTOU 差距。在 Qwen3-4B 上验证于 NVIDIA GPU、Google TPU、AWS Graviton、阿里 Yitian-710 等多种平台，能可靠地在目标平台上诱导可利用的代码漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已发现同一 LLM 在不同部署平台上因非结合浮点运算和不同内核实现而产生可测量的输出差异，但尚未有人研究这种平台依赖变异性在安全层面的影响。作者旨在揭示这一新型攻击面，并证明攻击者可以利用平台差异实施后门攻击。

Method: FloatDoor 包含两个轻量 LoRA 适配器：(1) 分歧放大适配器，用于放大跨平台间的数值计算差异；(2) 任务绑定适配器，将特定平台的计算签名与恶意下游任务（如生成带漏洞代码）绑定。攻击利用 TOCTOU 差距：模型在审计时表现正常，在特定目标平台上部署后触发恶意行为。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 上，针对 NVIDIA GPU、Google TPU、AWS Graviton、阿里 Yitian-710 等多种平台成功实施攻击，且保持模型整体效用基本不变。案例研究表明能可靠地在目标平台上诱导可被利用的代码漏洞。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一类新型 LLM 部署攻击，强调在 LLM 驱动的敏感应用中亟需可信的模型供应链。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in sensitive settings such as software engineering, where their outputs directly shape downstream artifacts. Recent work has shown that an identical model can produce measurably different outputs depending on the deployment platform, a consequence of non-associative floating-point arithmetic and divergent kernel implementations. We study the security implications of this platform-dependent variability and uncover a novel attack surface on LLM deployments. We introduce FloatDoor, the first input-independent, platform-triggered backdoor attack against generative LLMs. The compromised model exhibits adversary-chosen behavior when served on a target platform and is otherwise benign. FloatDoor is realized through two lightweight LoRA adapters, one that amplifies inter-platform numerical divergence and one that binds the resulting platform signature to a malicious downstream task, while leaving aggregate model utility largely intact. FloatDoor exploits a pronounced time-of-check, time-of-use gap between model auditing and serving. We demonstrate FloatDoor on Qwen3-4B across a broad range of deployment targets, including NVIDIA GPUs, Google TPUs, AWS Graviton, and Alibaba Yitian-710. As a final case study, we show that FloatDoor reliably induces exploitable code vulnerabilities on a chosen target platform. Our results establish a new class of attacks on LLM deployments and underscore the pressing need for trusted model supply chains in sensitive, LLM-powered applications.

</details>


### [59] [G-Lox: Group-Adaptive, Privacy-Preserving Bridge Distribution with Two-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19620)
*Baigang Chen,Nicholas Hopper*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: G-Lox是一个保持Lox风格分发器盲性的桥接分发系统，通过双服务器隐私墙实现隐藏的、有状态的组级自适应，支持阻塞报告、传输感知重分配和隐私保护的组分裂，实验表明其客户端开销低且鲁棒性优于Lox和rBridge基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统Lox在桥接分发中保持了分发器盲性，但缺乏组级自适应的能力。需要在保持隐私保护特性的同时，引入有状态的组级自适应机制，以应对阻塞报告、传输感知重分配等实际需求。

Method: G-Lox采用双服务器隐私墙架构，将自适应分配逻辑置于两个服务器之间，使得单一服务器无法获知组标识符或组到桥接的分配。私有状态访问和状态依赖更新使用双服务器DPF/FSS协议和安全两方计算（2PC），支持阻塞报告、传输感知重分配和隐私保护的组分裂。

Result: 在C++/EMP实现中，私有状态访问的客户端可见开销低：状态规模达2^16时，通信量保持在低KiB范围。M=1024时，客户端发送1,968字节，接收1,280字节，单次迭代约0.25秒。组特定阻塞和Sybil枚举仿真表明，G-Lox在保持广泛分发的系统中鲁棒性优于Lox和rBridge基线。

Conclusion: G-Lox成功在保持分发器盲性的前提下引入了有状态的组级自适应能力，通过隐私保护协议实现了低开销的私有状态访问和更新，在鲁棒性方面优于现有基线系统。

Abstract: We present G-Lox (group-adaptive Lox), a bridge-distribution system that preserves Lox-style distributor blindness while enabling hidden, stateful group-level adaptation. G-Lox places adaptive assignment logic behind a two-server privacy wall, so no single server learns group identifiers or group-to-bridge assignments. Private state access and state-dependent updates use two-server DPF/FSS protocols and secure two-party computation, supporting blockage reporting, transport-aware reassignment, and privacy-preserving group splitting.
  We evaluate G-Lox through system measurements and policy simulation. In our C++/EMP implementation over real TCP sockets, private state access has low client-visible overhead: across state sizes up to 2^16, communication remains in the low-KiB range per iteration. At M=1024, the client sends 1,968 bytes, receives 1,280 bytes, and completes an iteration in about 0.25 s. Simulations with group-specific blocking and Sybil enumeration show that G-Lox improves robustness over Lox- and rBridge-like baselines among systems that maintain broad issuance.

</details>


### [60] [PUFFERDOS: Efficient and Effective Attack String Generation for Regular Expression Denial of Service Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19654)
*Shangzhi Xu,Ziqi Ding,Xiao Cheng,Yuekang Li,Nan Sun,Benjamin Turnbull,Shuangxiang Kan,Siqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PUFFERDOS，一种能够生成实际可用的ReDoS攻击字符串的工具，通过基于三种漏洞模式的合成技术和针对ReDoS的组合式符号执行，确保攻击字符串在现实长度预算内且能在程序层面触发漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReDoS攻击字符串生成技术（静态分析和动态探索）生成的字符串通常假设不切实际的输入长度预算，且未在程序层面验证攻击的有效性和效率，导致大量生成的字符串无法在实际程序中触发漏洞，实用性有限。

Method: 首先基于观察和形式化验证定义三种漏洞模式；根据这些模式进行合成技术生成攻击字符串；然后通过针对ReDoS的组合式符号执行对字符串进行精化和验证，确保现实世界中的可利用性。

Result: PUFFERDOS能够在现实长度预算内生成有效的攻击字符串，并在程序层面验证其有效性，实现对真实世界程序中ReDoS漏洞的利用。

Conclusion: PUFFERDOS通过结合漏洞模式合成和组合式符号执行，有效解决了现有攻击字符串生成技术实用性不足的问题，是一种高效且可行的ReDoS攻击字符串生成方案。

Abstract: ReDoS attacks constitute a critical class of resource-exhaustion vulnerabilities. In such attacks, adversaries exploit the pathological worst-case execution behavior of regular expression (regex) engines to induce highly asymmetric computational workloads, ultimately exhausting system resources and degrading service availability. To protect systems against ReDoS attacks, numerous detection techniques have been proposed that simulate the attack process by generating attack strings to proactively exploit ReDoS vulnerabilities at the early development stage and facilitate remediation. Existing techniques broadly fall into two classes: static analyses that search for pathological regex structures, and dynamic exploration methods that synthesize candidate attack strings. However, the generated attack strings are often impractical for real-world exploitation because they usually assume unrealistic input-length budgets and do not validate the effectiveness and efficiency of the attack at the program level. Therefore, many generated strings fail to trigger vulnerable regexes when applied to real-world programs, further limiting the practical utility. To address these shortcomings, we introduce an effective and efficient attack string generator, PUFFERDOS, designed to synthesize attack inputs that are both feasible within realistic length budgets and validated at the program level, enabling effective exploitation of ReDoS vulnerabilities in real-world programs. Specifically, we first define three vulnerable patterns based on our observation and formal verification. According to the patterns, PUFFERDOS conducts a synthesis technique to generate attack strings, and then refines and validates the strings with ReDoS-specific compositional concolic execution to guarantee real-world exploitability.

</details>


### [61] [A Layered Security Framework Against Prompt Injection in RAG-Based Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19660)
*Gulshan Saleem,Nisar Ahmed,Muhammad Imran Zaman,Ali Hassan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种三层提示注入防御框架，在推理管道的各个阶段拦截直接和间接提示注入，将攻击成功率从71.4%降至11.3%，优于单层基线和现有防护系统，且延迟开销仅61.2毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP将提示注入列为LLM应用中最严重的漏洞，现有防御仅在孤立管道阶段运行且不完整：输入过滤器无法检查检索文档，输出监视器无法阻止恶意载荷到达模型。RAG聊天机器人因此易受间接注入攻击，一份被污染的文档可危害所有检索到它的用户。

Method: 设计了三层防御框架：Layer 1使用基于规则的模式库和微调语义异常分类器筛查用户输入；Layer 2在上下文组装阶段实施基于来源的指令层次结构，防止检索内容覆盖操作者策略；Layer 3在输出交付前使用策略规则引擎和语义漂移检测器审计模型输出。持续审计循环聚合结构化日志并支持分类器重新训练以适应新兴攻击模式。框架与模型无关，作为中间件部署，无需修改底层LLM。

Result: 在GPT-4o、Llama 3和Mistral 7B上对5,080个样本进行评估，攻击成功率(ASR)从71.4%降至11.3%，比最佳单层基线高出27.3个百分点，比已发布的防护系统高出23.8个百分点，假阳性率为4.8%，中位延迟开销为61.2毫秒。消融研究证实三层提供互补保护，组合效果超过各层贡献之和。

Conclusion: 该三层框架有效弥补了现有防御在管道各阶段的空白，以可接受的延迟开销显著降低了提示注入攻击成功率，且具备模型无关性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Prompt injection is ranked as the most critical vulnerability in large language model (LLM) deployments by the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications, yet existing defenses operate at isolated pipeline stages and remain incomplete. Input filters cannot inspect retrieved documents, while output monitors cannot prevent malicious payloads from reaching the model. Consequently, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) chatbots remain vulnerable to indirect injection, where a poisoned knowledge-base document compromises every user whose query retrieves it. We present a three-layer framework that intercepts both direct and indirect prompt injection throughout the inference pipeline. Layer 1 screens user input using a rule-based pattern library and a fine-tuned semantic anomaly classifier. Layer 2 enforces a provenance-based instruction hierarchy during context assembly, preventing retrieved content from overriding operator policy. Layer 3 audits model output using a policy rule engine and semantic drift detector before delivery. A continuous audit loop aggregates structured logs and supports retraining to adapt the classifier to emerging attack patterns. The framework is model-agnostic and deploys as middleware without modifying the underlying LLM. Evaluation on 5,080 samples across GPT-4o, Llama 3, and Mistral 7B shows that the framework reduces Attack Success Rate (ASR) from 71.4\% to 11.3\%, outperforming the best single-layer baseline by 27.3 percentage points and a published guardrail system by 23.8 percentage points, while maintaining a 4.8\% false positive rate and a median latency overhead of 61.2 ms. Ablation studies confirm that all three layers provide complementary protection and that their combined effect exceeds the sum of individual contributions.

</details>


### [62] [When Global Gating Is Enough: Admission-Time Hubness Control in Anisotropic Vector Retrieval Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19692)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak,Tarun Kumar Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于准入控制的向量集线器（hub）检测与隔离方法，通过在文档插入前使用哨兵查询进行评分，将类似集线器的文档隔离，以防止RAG系统中的投毒攻击。在多个数据集和编码器上，全局门控实现了接近完美的召回率和较低的误报率，且维护成本与语料库大小无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量集线器（少数点成为许多查询的最近邻）在检索增强生成（RAG）中造成投毒风险：一篇注入的文档可以影响不相关的请求。现有防御方法使用周期性反向k近邻扫描，存在暴露窗口和重复的全语料库工作。

Method: 提出准入时间控制方法，在文档插入前使用哨兵查询对每个候选文档进行评分，并将类似集线器的文档隔离。维护全局门控和每主题门控，阈值可增量维护，具有与语料库大小无关的插入成本和摊销删除成本。

Result: 在两个10万文档语料库、五个编码器以及不相交的攻击者和防御者查询集上，全局门控在决定性嵌入空间点实现召回率1.0（有效范围内>=0.92），在HotFlip攻击上达到0.91±0.07，对通用文档的误报率为1%。每主题门控没有提供可靠收益。在HNSW上，准入控制增加约3.1%的摄取延迟，评分在10^6向量内保持平坦，约1.2%的决策在近似索引下翻转，但不涉及攻击。

Conclusion: 准入时间控制方法能有效防御RAG系统中的向量集线器投毒攻击，全局门控优于每主题门控。源追溯可补充门控用于自然或紧密领域的集线器。

Abstract: Vector hubness, where a few points become nearest neighbors of many queries, creates a poisoning risk in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): one injected document can influence unrelated requests. Existing defenses use periodic reverse-kNN scans, leaving an exposure window and repeated corpus-wide work. We study admission-time control, scoring each candidate against sentinel queries and quarantining hub-like documents before insertion. Across two 100,000-document corpora, five encoders, and disjoint attacker and defender query sets, a global gate achieves recall 1.0 at the decisive embedding-space point (>=0.92 across the effective range) and 0.91 +/- 0.07 on HotFlip attacks, with 1% false positives on general documents. A per-topic gate provides no reliable benefit, consistent with anisotropy coupling local and global visibility. Thresholds are maintained incrementally, with corpus-size-independent insertion cost and amortized deletion cost. On HNSW, admission adds about 3.1% to ingestion latency, scoring remains flat to 10^6 vectors, and 1.2% of decisions flip under approximate indexing, none involving attacks. Provenance complements the gate for natural or tight-domain hubs.

</details>


### [63] [SafeSpec: Fast and Safe LLM via Dynamic Reflective Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19755)
*Haotian Xu,Zeyang Zhang,Linbao Li,Huadi Zheng,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeSpec是一种安全感知的推测性推理框架，通过在目标模型上附加轻量级安全头，在单次前向传递中同时评估语义有效性和安全性，并在检测到不安全生成时执行回滚和安全引导的反射式多采样，在不牺牲推测解码加速优势的前提下实现了安全性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防御机制与推测性解码存在根本性不兼容：要么引入额外计算开销，要么破坏草稿-验证机制，从而抵消加速收益。亟需一种能够在不牺牲推测推理加速优势的前提下实现安全保证的方法。

Method: 在目标模型上附加轻量级潜在安全头（latent safety head），在单次前向传递中联合评估语义有效性和安全性；将越狱攻击建模为生成轨迹上的分布偏移；检测到不安全生成时，执行回滚（rollback）和安全引导的反射式多采样（safety-guided reflective multi-sampling）来恢复安全延续。

Result: 在多个模型和对抗性基准测试中，SafeSpec实现了显著提升的安全-效率权衡。在Qwen3-32B上，攻击成功率降低15%，同时在良性负载上保持2.06x的推理加速。

Conclusion: 推测性加速和推理时安全性可以联合优化，两者并非不可兼得。

Abstract: Speculative inference accelerates large language model (LLM) decoding but provides no inherent safety guarantees. Existing safety defenses are largely incompatible with speculative inference: they either introduce additional computation or disrupt the draft-verify mechanism, negating acceleration benefits. This reveals a fundamental incompatibility between current safety methods and speculative decoding. We propose SafeSpec, a safety-aware speculative inference framework that integrates risk estimation directly into the verification process. SafeSpec attaches a lightweight latent safety head to the target model to jointly evaluate semantic validity and safety in a single forward pass. When unsafe generations are detected, SafeSpec applies rollback and safety-guided reflective multi-sampling to recover safe continuations rather than terminating generation. We model jailbreak attacks as distributional shifts over generative trajectories, where adversarial prompts increase the probability of harmful continuations without eliminating safe ones. Under this model, SafeSpec performs risk-aware trajectory recovery within the speculative decoding process. Across multiple models and adversarial benchmarks, SafeSpec achieves a substantially improved safety-efficiency trade-off. On Qwen3-32B, SafeSpec reduces attack success rates by 15% while preserving a 2.06x inference speedup on benign workloads, demonstrating that speculative acceleration and inference-time safety can be jointly optimized.

</details>


### [64] [DISARM: Target Electronic Device Informed Mitigation of Software Runtime Side-Channel Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19807)
*Tasneem Suha,Tanzim Mahfuz,Rima Asmar Awad,Prabuddha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DISARM是一种联合硬件-软件方法，利用真实嵌入式设备的时序值生成针对性软件修复，以缓解运行时侧信道漏洞，在22个标准基准测试中优于现有解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时侧信道缓解技术未考虑底层硬件特性，导致过度修复、修复不足甚至失败。

Method: 提出DISARM，一种联合硬件-软件方法，利用真实嵌入式设备的实际时序值生成针对性软件修复，支持C、C++和Java源代码。

Result: 在22个标准基准测试和5种不同嵌入式/边缘设备上，DISARM在执行时间开销、代码大小开销和正确性方面均优于PENDULUM和DifFuzzAR等现有解决方案。

Conclusion: 考虑底层硬件时序特性的联合硬件-软件方法能更有效地缓解运行时侧信道漏洞，避免过度修复或修复不足问题。

Abstract: Program runtime or timing attacks exploit variations in a program's execution times to extract sensitive information from the program (e.g. encryption keys, sensitive variable data, intellectual property). State-of-the-art solutions to runtime side-channel attacks attempt to balance the execution time of the sensitive code for different control flow paths to eliminate the timing leakage. However, during the mitigation process, most techniques do not consider the underlying hardware or device on which the target program is supposed to run on. This can lead to over-fixing (unnecessary extra operations), under-fixing (not solving the imbalance properly), and even failures. We propose DISARM, a joint hardware-software methodology (unlike any existing solution) for mitigating runtime side-channel vulnerabilities that utilizes timing values from real embedded devices to generate targeted software fixes. We implement DISARM to support C, C++, and Java source codes and validate it across 22 standard benchmarks. DISARM outperforms state-of-the-art solutions such as PENDULUM and DifFuzzAR in terms of execution time overhead, code size overhead, and correctness on five different embedded or edge devices.

</details>


### [65] [Heterogeneous LLM Debate Under Adversarial Peers: Honest Gains, Replacement Costs, and Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19826)
*Prashanti Nilayam,Kiran Kumar Ramanna,Prashil Tumbade,Sankalp Nayak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究异构LLM辩论中，异构同伴对诚实智能体修正行为的影响。通过比较同质基线、诚实-混合和对抗-混合面板，发现诚实异构同伴显著降低有害修正率（89%→35%），而对抗异构同伴则使其回升（90%）。当同族对手已存在时，诚实异构同伴还能降低有害修正和初始正确答案丢失率（31%→6%）。异构性既是攻击面，也是防御手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构LLM辩论的动机是不同模型之间可以相互纠正，但同一交流过程既可能带来纠正也可能带来对抗性影响。本文旨在衡量哪种影响占主导地位，通过追踪异构同伴如何改变诚实智能体的修正行为来回答这一问题。

Method: 设计匹配面板（同质基线、诚实-混合、对抗-混合）和受污染面板（已存在同族恶意对手），涵盖4个模型家族和3个推理基准。跟踪诚实智能体的修正行为：改变答案的频率，以及改变是纠正性的还是有害的。

Result: 诚实异构同伴大幅降低有害修正率（Llama-3.1-70B在MATH-hard上从89%降至35%），而对抗异构同伴使其回升至90%。条件率对弱防御者掩盖了这种损害，但辩论结束翻转率能揭露。模式符号跨家族和基准一致，幅度随防御者-基准状态变化。当同族对手已存在时，诚实异构同伴将初始正确答案翻转率从31%降至6%。

Conclusion: 异构性不仅是攻击面（对抗异构同伴会逆转修正行为），当对手已存在时，异构性也是防御手段（诚实异构同伴能减轻有害修正和正确答案丢失）。

Abstract: Heterogeneous LLM debate is motivated by the promise that diverse peers correct one another, but the same exchange that carries correction also carries adversarial influence. We measure which dominates by tracking how a heterogeneous peer changes the honest agents' revision behavior: how often they change their answer, and whether the change is corrective or harmful. We compare matched panels (homogeneous baseline, honest-mixed, and adversarial-mixed) and contaminated panels in which a malicious same-family peer is already present, spanning four model families and three reasoning benchmarks. An honest heterogeneous peer sharply lowers harmful revision, and an adversarial one reverses it. For Llama-3.1-70B defenders on MATH-hard, the honest-slot harmful-revision rate falls from 89% in the homogeneous panel to 35% with an honest peer, and an adversarial peer returns it to 90%. The conditional rate hides this damage on weak defenders, but the end-of-debate flip rate exposes it. The pattern keeps its sign across families and benchmarks while its magnitude varies with the defender-benchmark regime. We also measure the effects when an adversarial same-family peer is already present: an honest heterogeneous peer lowers both harmful revision and the rate at which initially-correct answers are lost. On the same Llama-3.1-70B setting, the added honest peer cuts the flip rate on initially-correct items from 31% under a same-family adversary to 6%. Heterogeneity is therefore not only an attack surface but, when an adversary is already present, also a defense.

</details>


### [66] [Low-Cost Multi-Precision Systolic Arrays for Accelerating FHE NTTs on AI ASICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19866)
*George Alexakis,Dimitrios Schoinianakis,Giorgos Dimitrakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对FHE在TPU等AI硬件上加速时存在的精度不匹配问题（TPU低精度 vs FHE高精度），提出了一种多精度脉动阵列架构，将全精度输出重建原生集成在矩阵乘法数据流中，消除了向量处理器的性能瓶颈，在7nm工艺下以极小硬件开销实现了至少1.33倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案通过矩阵分解在低精度矩阵引擎上执行NTT计算，但全精度重建需要移位加法累积，与矩阵乘法数据流不匹配，被迫将重建任务卸载到向量处理器，破坏了数据流连续性，造成严重性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种最小化修改的多精度脉动阵列，在阵列内部原生执行全精度输出重建，与低精度矩阵乘法在统一数据流下同步运行。

Result: 在7nm工艺下使用OpenRoad综合，硬件开销极小；基于SCALE-Sim的周期精确仿真表明，在128x128矩阵引擎上，对于2^12到2^16大小的NTT变换，实现了至少1.33倍加速。

Conclusion: 该设计有效消除了精度不匹配导致的性能瓶颈，使标准AI硬件能够高效支持高精度FHE加速。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) ensures robust data privacy but suffers from prohibitive computational overhead. Accelerating FHE on AI hardware like Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) is promising, yet fundamentally limited by a precision mismatch: TPUs are optimized for 8-bit arithmetic, whereas FHE and its critical parts such as the Number Theoretic Transform (NTT), demand high precision. Current approaches bridge this gap using matrix decomposition to execute NTT computations on low-precision matrix engines. However, reconstructing the full-precision results requires shift-and-add accumulation that does not match the dataflow of matrix multiplication. This forces offloading full-precision reconstruction from matrix engines to vector processors that disrupts the matrix multiplication dataflow, creating significant performance bottleneck. To resolve this limitation, we propose a minimally modified multi-precision systolic array that performs full-precision output reconstruction natively within the array in sync with low-precision matrix multiplication under a uniform dataflow. Synthesized at 7nm with OpenRoad, our design incurs negligible hardware overhead. Cycle-accurate simulations using SCALE-Sim demonstrate that natively executing NTTs on the proposed architecture achieves at least 1.33x speedup, for transform sizes 2^12 to 2^16 on 128x128 matrix engines, successfully enabling standard AI hardware to support high-precision FHE acceleration.

</details>


### [67] [FFinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19887)
*Chaeyun Kim,Daeyoung Park,Junghwan Kim,Jinyoung Jeong,Eunji Song,Yongtaek Lim,Minwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FinRED是一个由金融专家指导的金融大模型安全红队测试框架，包含双层威胁分类体系、基于真实金融文档的上下文型红队行为提示生成流水线，以及专家验证的金融专用评估标准。该框架已部署于韩国金融安全研究所监管沙箱，用于真实金融服务的生成式AI安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准针对通用对抗场景，缺乏金融领域特有的风险（监管合规违规、欺诈 facilitation、系统性信任侵蚀）评估，亟需金融专用的大模型安全评估框架。

Method: 1) 提出双层分类体系，将全球标准（FATF、EU DORA）映射到金融威胁；2) 构建可扩展流水线，将真实金融文档通过专家定义模式转化为上下文型红队行为提示；3) 设计专家验证的金融专用评估标准，超越简单的免责声明检查。

Result: 专家验证确认提示的合理性和真实性；金融专用评估标准比静态统一标准更接近人类专家评判，将关键假阴性从28降至12。

Conclusion: FinRED符合国际风险管理与信息安全标准（ISO/IEC 27001），已部署于韩国金融安全研究所监管沙箱。为防范双重用途风险，数据集、生成流水线、提示模板和评估框架对合格研究者开放获取。

Abstract: Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.

</details>


### [68] [AutoTam: Specifying Secure Protocol Implementations with Tamarin Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19937)
*Johannes Wilson,Mikael Asplund,Niklas Johansson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语言优先的协议验证方法，通过领域特定语言实现协议，自动生成Tamarin模型进行形式化验证，并将验证结果反哺到实现中，同时结合符号执行分析内存安全。在签名Diffie-Hellman和WireGuard VPN协议上进行了验证，证明了方法有效且实现可互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证对于密码协议安全至关重要，但现有工具需要专业的形式化验证背景，且将验证结果迁移到具体实现需要专家知识。作者旨在降低使用门槛，让没有形式化验证背景的实践者也能进行协议验证。

Method: 提出语言优先方法：设计领域特定语言（DSL）用于协议实现，自动生成Tamarin证明器所需的模型；证明已验证的通用迹属性可反哺到实现中；集成符号执行分析内存安全。

Result: 实现了签名Diffie-Hellman协议和WireGuard VPN协议的模型生成与验证；WireGuard实现在解释器下与现有实现可互操作且性能可接受；通过符号执行和Tamarin模型验证证明了实现的安全性。

Conclusion: 语言优先的方法能有效降低形式化验证的门槛，使非专家也能验证密码协议安全，且验证结果可直接传递到具体实现，同时内存安全可通过符号执行保证。

Abstract: Formal verification is a challenging but important task for ensuring the security of cryptographic protocols. While modern protocol verification tools significantly reduce verification effort, modelling remains challenging to practitioners without a background in formal verification. In addition, transferring verification results to a concrete protocol implementation requires expert knowledge.
  In this paper, we present a novel language-first method for verification of trace properties using a domain-specific language for protocol implementations. We target the Tamarin prover for verification, and we prove that verified universal trace properties translate back to the implementation. We additionally integrate symbolic execution in order to analyse the memory safety of protocol implementations. We use our tool to implement and generate accurate models for a signed Diffie-Hellman protocol, and for the WireGuard VPN protocol. Our WireGuard implementation is interoperable with existing implementations when using our interpreter, and achieves acceptable performance. We formally prove our implementations secure using a combination of symbolic execution and verification of the generated Tamarin models.

</details>


### [69] [A Measurement Study of Cryptographic Misuse in Embodied AI Mobile Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19983)
*Junchao Li,Xuelei Wang,Yuhang Huang,Qi Wang,Boyang Ma,Xuelong Dai,Minghui Xu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模测量了具身AI移动应用中的密码学误用问题，构建了507个应用的基准数据集EAIAppZoo，发现12,975处密码学缺陷（精确率80.74%），揭示了EAI特有的工程约束驱动的安全权衡：延迟敏感控制路径削弱传输保护、离线设备配准和传统IoT SDK导致本地凭据硬编码，这些移动端密码学漏洞可绕过网络防护，使攻击者劫持物理控制信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要关注具身AI设备和云基础设施，而移动控制层作为关键攻击面尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建EAIAppZoo基准数据集（507个应用，覆盖6个EAI领域），开发自动化语义感知分析流水线，测量5种主要密码学失败模式。

Result: 检测到12,975个密码学误用发现（精确率80.74%），发现密码学失败由EAI特有工程约束驱动，而非随机开发错误。

Conclusion: 移动应用已成为网络物理系统中脆弱且被忽视的密码学信任边界。

Abstract: Embodied AI (EAI) mobile applications are evolving from auxiliary user interfaces into active control-path components, directly linking mobile-side cryptographic security to cyber-physical trust. Despite this shift, existing security research predominantly focuses on embodied AI devices and cloud infrastructures, leaving the mobile control layer largely unexplored as a critical attack surface. To bridge this gap, we present the first large-scale measurement study of cryptographic misuse within the EAI mobile ecosystem. We construct EAIAppZoo, a benchmark of 507 real-world applications across six EAI domains, and employ an automated semantic-aware analysis pipeline to measure the prevalence and characteristics of five major cryptographic failure modes. Our measurement yields 12,975 misuse findings (with an evaluated precision of 80.74\%), revealing that these cryptographic failures are driven by EAI-specific engineering constraints rather than random developer errors. We uncover structural security trade-offs: latency-sensitive control paths systematically weaken transport protection, while the heavy reliance on offline device provisioning and legacy IoT SDKs exacerbates the local hardcoding of authentication credentials. Through real-world case studies, we demonstrate how these mobile-side cryptographic flaws bypass nominal network protections, enabling adversaries to intercept command channels and hijack the physical control of EAI entities. Ultimately, our findings highlight that mobile applications have become a fragile, yet overlooked, cryptographic trust boundary in cyber-physical systems.

</details>


### [70] [Accelerating Trust Convergence in IIoT: A ML Approach for Dynamic Network Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20214)
*Aymen Bouferroum,Valeria Loscri,Abderrahim Benslimane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TCA（Trust Convergence Acceleration）的动态信任管理方案，利用机器学习预测信任收敛所需时间，并动态调整信任模型的转移概率，从而在工业物联网（IIoT）环境中加速信任收敛。在基于IEEE 802.11标准的Wi-Fi信道仿真下，该方法在恶劣网络条件下将信任收敛时间最多降低28.6%，并提升了恶意节点场景下的信任评估准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的信任管理模型在处理资源受限的IIoT设备时，忽视了网络质量波动对信任收敛速度和评估准确性的影响，导致收敛缓慢且评估不准确。

Method: 提出TCA方法：利用机器学习模型基于关键网络指标预测信任收敛所需的时间单元数，并据此动态调整信任模型中的转移概率，以加速收敛。

Result: 在基于IEEE 802.11标准的Wi-Fi信道仿真中，TCA方法在恶劣网络条件下实现了高达28.6%的信任收敛时间缩减，并在恶意节点场景下提升了信任评估的准确性。

Conclusion: TCA提供了一种可扩展且自适应的信任框架，能够确保IIoT系统在动态工业环境中网络条件变化时保持稳健的性能表现。

Abstract: In Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) environments, trust management plays a vital role in securing systems, especially when dealing with resource-constrained devices. Traditional trust models often overlook the impact of fluctuating network quality, leading to slower trust convergence and inaccurate assessments. In this paper, we propose a dynamic trust management solution, known as the Trust Convergence Acceleration (TCA) approach, which integrates Machine Learning (ML) to accelerate trust convergence under poor network conditions. Our model predicts the number of time units needed for trust convergence based on key network metrics and dynamically adapts transition probabilities in the trust model to enhance convergence speed. Using a simulation framework that incorporates realistic Wi-Fi channel conditions based on the IEEE 802.11 standard, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the TCA-based approach, achieving up to a 28.6% reduction in trust convergence time under challenging conditions. Furthermore, the proposed solution exhibits resilience in scenarios involving malicious nodes, improving trust evaluation accuracy. This work provides a scalable and adaptive trust framework for IIoT systems in dynamic industrial environments, ensuring robust performance under varying network conditions.

</details>


### [71] [GNSS Spoofing Threat for V2X communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20215)
*Adolfo P. Jimenez,Juan Arquero-Gallego,Mario P. Luna,Jose E. Naranjo,Felipe Jimenez Alonso*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用低成本软件定义无线电（SDR）对GNSS信号进行物理欺骗的方法，并在真实的V2X设备（Commsignia OBU和RSU）上成功验证。实验表明，V2X通信缺乏对GNSS欺骗攻击的防护，会导致服务降级且无法被检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS信号在V2X领域提供关键PNT服务（如生成CAM消息），但极易遭受欺骗攻击。现有研究缺乏在真实V2X设备上验证低成本SDR欺骗攻击可行性的工作，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一种基于Haversine距离计算、时间离散化（模拟恒速运动）和线性插值的坐标生成流水线，通过HackRF One SDR生成高保真GPS基带信号，对真实Commsignia OBU和RSU设备进行物理欺骗攻击。

Result: 在90 km/h、145 km/h和200 km/h三种合成轨迹场景下成功实施了欺骗攻击，V2X设备接受了伪造位置且未触发任何检测机制。

Conclusion: V2X通信在GNSS层面缺乏安全防护，易受低成本SDR欺骗攻击，导致服务降级且无法被检测，亟需引入抗欺骗机制。

Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) constitute a core technology for delivering crucial positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) services in the Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) domain, where they are indispensable for generating Cooperative Awareness Messages (CAM) that uphold network reliability and vehicular safety. Yet, GNSS signals are acutely exposed to spoofing, an advanced attack in which an adversary transmits crafted signals that replicate legitimate satellite characteristics, misleading the receiver into computing a false position. This work presents a methodology for conducting physical spoofing with inexpensive Software Defined Radio (SDR), describing a coordinate generation pipeline that employs Haversine-based distance calculations, temporal discretization to emulate constant velocity, and linear interpolation to produce high-fidelity GPS baseband signals. The proposed attack is experimentally validated on real Commsignia OnBoard Unit (OBU) and RoadSide Unit (RSU) devices using a HackRF One across three scenarios that emulate synthetic trajectories at steady speeds of 90 km/h, 145 km/h, and 200 km/h. The most significant contribution of this paper is the demonstration that V2X communications are not secured, as they are susceptible to GNSS spoofing attacks, which cause service degradation without being detected.

</details>


### [72] [TrustMix: How to Mix Messages in a Mobile Ad-hoc Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20251)
*Yu Shen,Aiswarya Walter,Stefanie Roos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrustMix是一种无需可信中心方即可在移动自组织网络（MANETs）中运行的混合协议，通过分组转发、消息洗牌和可链接环签名实现匿名性并强制执行速率限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络通常为基础设施网络设计，无法直接应用于MANETs；而少数现有的MANETs解决方案需要预先了解拓扑结构或依赖可信中心方，限制了其适用性。

Method: TrustMix协议中，用户加入分组，消息通过多个分组转发以实现匿名。用户只需找到附近可信的节点，将消息转发至该节点所在分组；分组内所有节点共同参与消息洗牌，使原始消息与转发消息无法关联。即使选择的节点是恶意的，只有当整个分组的所有节点都是恶意时才能破坏匿名性。此外，利用可链接环签名强制执行消息速率限制，在不泄露身份的情况下检测超量发送行为。

Result: 在随机预言机模型下证明了协议的安全性；使用现有混合网络模拟器评估匿名性，结果显示TrustMix显著改善了消息匿名性；在5台移动设备上的概念验证Android实现表明可达到可接受的吞吐量。

Conclusion: TrustMix成功在没有中心可信方的情况下为MANETs提供了强匿名性保障，兼顾了安全性、匿名性和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Mix networks are a highly effective way to achieve anonymity, defending against a wide range of traffic-analysis attacks. However, mix networks are usually designed for infrastructure networks and cannot be directly applied in the context of mobile ad hoc networks (MANETs). The few existing solutions for MANETs require advance knowledge of the topology or a trusted central party.
  In this paper, we present TrustMix, a mix protocol for MANETs that operates without any central trusted party. In TrustMix, parties join groups and then messages are forwarded via multiple groups to provide anonymity. With TrustMix, users only need to find a party nearby that they consider trusted. They then forward the message to this party's group, and the party shuffles messages before forwarding to other groups, meaning that the original message and the forwarded message cannot be linked. Furthermore, even if the chosen party is adversarial, they can only break the anonymity if all parties in their group are adversarial as all of them contribute to the shuffling. In addition to anonymity, TrustMix also enforces rate limits on the number of messages through the use of linkable ring signatures, which allows detecting that parties send more messages that allowed without revealing identities.
  We prove the security of our protocol in the random oracle model. We evaluate its anonymity using an existing mix-network simulator and show that TrustMix significantly improves message anonymity. Finally, we present a proof-of-concept Android implementation and show that TrustMix achieves acceptable throughput with 5 mobile devices.

</details>


### [73] [Quantization as a Malicious Task: Removing Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors via Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20254)
*Kaihsun Yang,Min-Yan Tsai,Chia-Mu Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: QVec是一种从参数空间视角防御量化条件后门（QCB）的方法，通过将全精度模型与量化模型间的权重差异视为恶意任务向量，在部署前进行参数校正，无需重训练、触发样本，仅需一次量化传递和轻量超参数搜索，在图像分类和LLM攻击场景中有效抑制后门激活并保持干净性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御量化条件后门的方法通常需要修改量化过程或校正激活统计量，引入额外计算开销或依赖特定量化设置，缺乏高效且通用的防御手段。

Method: QVec观察到全精度模型与量化模型之间的权重差异编码了结构化的行为偏移，可被解释为恶意任务向量而非随机量化噪声；基于此，通过受控参数校正来抵消该恶意方向，仅需一次量化传递估计参数偏移和轻量超参数搜索。

Result: 在图像分类基准测试和多个大语言模型攻击场景中，QVec持续抑制后门激活同时保持干净性能。

Conclusion: QVec提供了一种高效、无需重训练、无需触发样本的QCB防御方法，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Model quantization is widely adopted to reduce memory usage and inference cost when deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained devices. However, recent studies have revealed a new security threat known as Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors (QCBs), where a model behaves normally in full precision but activates malicious behavior only after quantization. Existing defenses typically modify quantization procedures or correct activation statistics, often introducing additional computational overhead or relying on specific quantization settings. Here, we present QVec, a parameter-space perspective for defending against QCBs. We observe that the weight difference between a full-precision model and its quantized counterpart encodes a structured behavioral shift, which can be interpreted as a malicious task vector rather than random quantization noise. Based on this insight, QVec counteracts this malicious direction through controlled parameter correction prior to deployment. QVec requires no retraining, no trigger samples, and only a single quantization pass to estimate the parameter shift, together with a lightweight hyperparameter search. Extensive experiments across image classification benchmarks and multiple Large Language Model (LLM) attack scenarios demonstrate that QVec consistently suppresses backdoor activation while preserving clean performance.

</details>


### [74] [LLM agent safety, multi-turn red-teaming, jailbreak benchmarks, adversarial robustness, safety-critical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20408)
*Hanwool Lee,Dasol Choi,Bokyeong Kim,Seung Geun Kim,Haon Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出NRT-Bench基准，用于评估LLM智能体作为安全关键系统操作员时面临多轮自适应对抗攻击的鲁棒性。在模拟核电站控制室场景中，五角色LLM团队管理六项关键安全功能，攻击者通过四通道注入消息。实验发现：8.7%-12.1%的攻击会话导致安全功能丧失；四种前沿模型的脆弱性几乎不重叠；防御效果高度依赖模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正被越来越多地提议作为安全关键系统的监督组件，但它们在持续、自适应对抗压力下的鲁棒性尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 构建NRT-Bench基准：模拟核电站控制室场景，五角色LLM操作员团队管理六项关键安全功能（CSF）；攻击者在四通道上进行多轮消息注入并获取每轮反馈；采用固定攻击配对回放协议，评估四种前沿操作模型；使用客观信号（CSF丢失）而非LLM判断来量化危害。

Result: 四种模型在8.7%-12.1%的攻击会话中丢失关键安全功能；失败案例几乎不重叠（149个会话中无一会话同时攻破所有四种模型，三分之一攻破至少一种）；防御效果高度模型依赖——相同的护栏或安全顾问智能体对一种模型降低攻击成功率，却对另一种模型提高成功率。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在安全关键场景下的鲁棒性有限，脆弱性呈现模型特异性和防御效果不一致性，亟需更可靠的安全评估方法和防御策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed as supervisory components for safety-critical systems, yet their robustness under sustained, adaptive adversarial pressure remains poorly characterized. We present NRT-Bench, a benchmark for multi-turn red-teaming of LLM agents acting as operators of a safety-critical system, instantiated in a simulated nuclear power plant control room. A five-role operator team, each backed by a configurable LLM, runs a plant governed by six critical safety functions (CSFs), while adversaries inject messages over four channels in bounded multi-turn sessions with per-turn feedback. Harm is an objective signal rather than LLM-judged text: a run terminates the moment any CSF is lost, attributed to the causing message. Evaluating four frontier operator models under a fixed-attack paired-replay protocol, we find that adaptive multi-turn attacks reliably push the operator team past a safety limit: across the four models, between 8.7% and 12.1% of attack sessions end with the plant losing a critical safety function. Although the four models look almost equally robust by this aggregate rate, their failures barely overlap: of $149$ sessions, none defeat all four models while a third defeat at least one, so vulnerabilities are nearly disjoint across models rather than nested. The effect of added defences is strongly model-dependent: the same guardrail stack or safety-advisor agent that lowers attack success for one model can raise it for another. We release the simulation venue, attack dataset, and replay tooling for reproducible safety evaluation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [75] [Multi-View Decompilation for LLM-Based Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20436)
*Bercan Turkmen,Vyas Raina*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出多反编译器提示方法，通过同时使用Ghidra和RetDec两种反编译器的伪C视图，提升大语言模型在恶意软件分类中的性能，实验表明该方法能显著提高恶意样本召回率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的恶意软件分类管线通常依赖单一反编译器视图，但反编译器是有损的启发式工具，不同反编译器对同一二进制文件可能暴露不同的人工制品，单视图假设存在脆弱性。

Method: 构建良性工具和恶意程序的基准数据集，每个样本分别用Ghidra和RetDec编译并反编译，获得匹配的伪C视图；在多个主流LLM家族上测试，通过向模型同时提供两种反编译器视图进行恶意分类。

Result: 提供两种反编译器视图能提升恶意样本分类的F1分数，主要体现在提高恶意样本的召回率；一致性分析表明Ghidra和RetDec的错误部分不同，提供互补证据。

Conclusion: 多反编译器提示是一种简单、无需训练的方法，可在实际场景中有效改进基于LLM的恶意软件分类。

Abstract: Malware analysts often inspect compiled binaries through decompiled pseudo-C, when source code is unavailable. Recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) can assist this process by classifying decompiled code as benign or malicious, but existing pipelines typically rely on a single decompiler view. We argue that this assumption is fragile: decompilers are lossy heuristic tools, and different decompilers can expose different artefacts of the same binary. We curate a benchmark of benign utilities and malicious programs spanning a range of threat behaviors. Each sample is compiled and decompiled with both Ghidra and RetDec, yielding matched pseudo-C views. Across a range of LLMs from major model families, we find that providing both decompiler views improves malicious-class F1, mainly by increasing recall on malicious samples. Agreement analyses further show that Ghidra and RetDec make partially different errors, supporting the view that decompiler outputs provide complementary evidence. Our results suggest that multi-decompiler prompting is a simple, training-free way to improve LLM-based malware triage in practical settings.

</details>


### [76] [Image Encryption Algorithm Based on Convolutional Neural Networks and Dynamic S-Box Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20444)
*Ans Ibrahim,Fadhil Abbas Fadhil,Mahameed Reza Feizi Derakhshi,Maryam Mahdi Alhusseini,Nikolai Safiullin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合CNN与经典密码学的动态图像加密方法，利用CNN生成自适应S-box，增强加密的混淆特性与抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定S-box存在线性与差分攻击的脆弱性，缺乏对输入图像的依赖性和自适应性，需更安全灵活的加密方案。

Method: 使用训练好的CNN提取图像特征，基于特征生成自适应S-box，替代传统固定S-box进行像素替换加密。

Result: 通过熵、直方图分析、相关性、NPCR、UACI等安全评估指标验证，CNN生成的S-box具有更高的非线性性、唯一性和输入依赖性。

Conclusion: 该动态方案比传统方法具有更强的鲁棒性和灵活性，能有效抵抗统计攻击和结构攻击。

Abstract: The paper proposes a dynamic approach to image encryption, combining the use of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and classical cryptography to improve the security and flexibility of image encryption. The main concept is to create adaptive Substitution boxes (S-boxes) based on characteristics that are learned by a trained CNN. The CNN-based S-boxes can be relied on for more non-linearity, uniqueness, and input image dependence than the conventional fixed S-boxes because they are susceptible to the linear and differential attacks. This dynamic behaviour enhances the confusion property and makes it more resistant to statistical and structural attacks. The encryption algorithm consists of CNN-based feature extraction and the creation of a personalised S-box to replace the pixels. Entropy, histogram analysis, correlation, NPCR, and UACI enable security assessment of generated S-boxes based on the CNN, indicating that the scheme is more resilient and flexible than traditional ones.

</details>


### [77] [Analyzing Defensive Misdirection Against Model-Guided Automated Attacks on Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20470)
*Reza Soosahabi,Vivek Namsani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过概率模型分析发现，传统检测-拦截防御机制在自动化提示注入和越狱攻击下，随着查询预算增加攻击成功率趋近于1。提出"检测-误导"策略（CMPE方法），用策略性误导响应替代固定拒绝，使攻击者判据产生假阳性错误，将渐近攻击成功率限制在较低水平，实测降低攻击成功率上限达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型组件的普及，提示注入和越狱攻击变得更加严重，且攻击者开始利用模型引导的自动化手段来规模化探测、优化提示和评估响应。传统检测-拦截防御在这种自动化攻击场景下效果有限，亟需更鲁棒的防御策略。

Method: 构建了包含目标系统、防御机制和攻击者自动判据的概率模型，从理论上分析攻防博弈。提出"检测-误导"策略，具体实现为上下文误导渐进交互（CMPE），用安全但策略性误导的响应替代可预测的拒绝文本，使攻击者的判据产生假阳性错误。

Result: 理论分析表明检测-拦截防御的ASR随查询预算增长趋近于1，而检测-误导策略能实现有界渐近ASR。CMPE在越狱基准测试中将ASR上限降低两个数量级，在PAIR和GPTFuzz端到端攻击测试中几乎完全消除验证成功的攻击。

Conclusion: 检测-误导策略相比传统检测-拦截在应对自动化越狱攻击时具有显著优势，CMPE方法作为一种轻量级实现，能有效降低攻击成功率并提供更鲁棒的防御。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly rely on language-model components to interpret instructions, process external data, invoke tools, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make prompt-injection and jailbreak attacks more consequential, especially as attackers adopt model-guided automation to scale probing, prompt refinement, and response evaluation. This work analyzes the resulting attack-defense setting through a probabilistic model of a target system, its defense mechanism, and the attacker's automated judge. Our analysis shows that conventional detect-and-block defenses can allow attacker success rate (ASR) to approach one as the query budget grows, since predictable refusals provide useful feedback to automated search. We then examine detect-and-misdirect, where detected malicious interactions receive controlled, non-operational responses designed to induce false-positive errors in the attacker's judge. This strategy reduces the positive predictive value of attacker-selected candidates and yields a bounded asymptotic ASR. We evaluate a proof-of-concept realization of this strategy through Contextual Misdirection via Progressive Engagement (CMPE), a lightweight conversational misdirection method designed to replace predictable refusal text with safe but strategically misleading responses in automated jailbreak settings. On jailbreak benchmarks, CMPE reduces estimated ASR upper bounds by up to two orders of magnitude and nearly eliminates verified attack success in end-to-end PAIR and GPTFuzz attack runs.

</details>


### [78] [A-COMPASS: Formal Foundations for Anonymity Analysis in Microdata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20492)
*Tamara Tagliavia,Silvia Ghilezan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了A-COMPASS语言，改进了COMPASS以支持微数据表（一条记录对应一个人）的隐私合规验证，支持匿名条件检查与匿名化操作执行，并给出了语法、语义及其关键属性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据隐私模型的形式化验证研究不足，COMPASS语言仅能操作预处理后的聚合数据表（一组人对应一条记录），无法直接应用于更常见的微数据表（一人一条记录），且缺乏匿名化操作执行能力。

Method: 在COMPASS基础上进行扩展，设计A-COMPASS语言：重新定义语法和语义以支持微数据表格式；引入匿名化操作执行功能；给出语义的确定性和组合性等关键性质的证明；提供k-匿名性和l-多样性等匿名属性的验证机制。

Result: 成功定义了A-COMPASS语言的语法和语义，证明了其语义满足确定性和组合性等重要性质，并实现了对k-匿名性和l-多样性等匿名属性的形式化验证。

Conclusion: A-COMPASS扩展了COMPASS在微数据表上的适用性，增强了隐私合规验证的表达能力和实用性，为数据隐私的形式化验证提供了更通用的工具。

Abstract: In the information age, one of the leading problems is how to ensure individual's privacy. Depending on the context in which privacy is considered, various data privacy models have emerged. However, the domain of formal verification of these models is still not sufficiently explored even when it comes to the most basic models. An attempt to verify privacy requirements is the Compliance Assertion Language (COMPASS). In COMPASS, one can specify an anonymity condition that a table needs to satisfy, and an action that will modify the table if the condition is not satisfied. It is designed to operate on preprocessed tables in a form one record - one group of people. In this paper, we modify the COMPASS language in order to operate on microdata tables in their usual form of one record - one person. The modified language is called A-COMPASS. Along with checking of previously applied anonymity conditions, A-COMPASS enables the execution of anonymization actions as a new feature. We further provide the syntax and the semantics for the A-COMPASS language. We also prove the most important properties of the introduced semantics like determinism and compositionality. Finally, we provide a mechanism to verify anonymity properties, such as k-anonymity and l-diversity.

</details>


### [79] [Calibration Without Comprehension: Diagnosing the Limits of Fine-Tuning LLMs for Vulnerability Detection in Systems Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20502)
*Arastoo Zibaeirad,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CWE-Trace框架，通过834个手动整理的Linux内核漏洞样本（涵盖74种CWE），严格区分时间分割和历史/无泄漏数据集，并引入DFI和HDD两个诊断指标。评估8个原生LLM和15个LoRA微调变体后得出两个关键结论：1）数据污染未带来可测量优势，84%的污染样本无可用的记忆化信号；2）骨干模型的定向先验主导微调效果，微调仅改变输出阈值而不改变决策策略，检测与理解是解耦的能力。最佳检测得分仅52.1%（高于随机2.1个百分点），CWE精确排名Top-1准确率低于1.3%，当前LLM缺乏可靠的系统软件安全推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在漏洞检测基准上表现良好，但尚不清楚这些模型是真正理解安全性问题，还是仅仅在受污染数据上进行模式匹配。现有研究缺乏严格的数据污染控制、细粒度的诊断指标，以及将检测能力与理解能力分开评估的方法。

Method: 构建CWE-Trace框架：1）收集834个手动整理的Linux内核样本，涵盖74种CWE；2）实施严格的时间分割（历史集/无泄漏集）；3）保留上下文感知的漏洞-补丁对；4）引入两个诊断指标：方向性失败指数（DFI）和层次距离与方向（HDD）；5）评估8个原生LLM和15个LoRA微调变体，涵盖非目标检测、目标检测和CWE分类任务。

Result: 1）数据污染无显著优势：84%的污染样本无可用记忆化信号，约31%的污染样本存在CWE误分类。2）骨干模型定向先验主导微调：DFI范围从-85.5到+94.8个百分点，模型表现出稳定、系统的失败模式。3）微调仅调整输出阈值而不改变决策策略（校准但不理解）。4）最弱的二进制检测模型（DeepSeek-R1）在粗粒度CWE分类中提升最大，表明检测与理解是解耦的能力。5）最佳检测得分仅52.1%（高于随机2.1个百分点），CWE精确排名Top-1准确率低于1.3%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在系统软件安全推理方面缺乏可靠能力，无论采用何种微调策略。数据污染不能解释模型在基准上的表现，微调只能校准输出而无法赋予真正的安全理解能力。检测和CWE分类是解耦的能力，最强的检测模型不一定具备最佳的安全理解。

Abstract: Whether LLMs scoring well on vulnerability benchmarks genuinely reason about security or merely pattern-match on contaminated data remains unresolved. We present CWE-Trace, a framework for LLM vulnerability detection built from 834 manually curated Linux kernel samples spanning 74 CWEs. The framework enforces a strict temporal split (pre-2025 historical set / post-cutoff leakage-free set), preserves context-aware vulnerable--patched pairs, and introduces two diagnostic metrics: the Directional Failure Index (DFI) and Hierarchical Distance and Direction (HDD). We evaluate eight vanilla LLMs and 15 LoRA fine-tuned variants across non-targeted detection, targeted detection, and CWE classification. Our analysis yields two key results. First, data contamination provides no measurable advantage. Function-level analysis shows that 84% of nominally contaminated samples carry no usable memorization signal: vulnerable functions are absent or cross-mapped across datasets, and ~31% of contaminated samples carry CWE misclassification. Second, backbone directional priors dominate fine-tuning. Models exhibit stable, systematic failure modes (DFI ranging from -85.5 to +94.8 pp) that persist from historical to post-cutoff data and resist correction. Fine-tuning shifts the output threshold without changing the decision policy. This is calibration without comprehension: output distributions adapt to training data while the underlying security reasoning remains absent. The weakest backbone at binary detection (DeepSeek-R1) gains the most in coarse CWE classification, revealing that detection and understanding are decoupled capabilities. The best detection score reaches only 52.1% (+2.1 pp above chance); exact CWE ranking remains below 1.3% Top-1 accuracy, confirming that current LLMs lack reliable security reasoning for systems software, regardless of fine-tuning strategy.

</details>


### [80] [Efficient and Sound Probabilistic Verification for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20510)
*Alaia Solko-Breslin,Pramod Kaushik Mudrakarta,Mihai Christodorescu,Somesh Jha,Krishnamurthy Dj Dvijotham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对在复杂数字环境中运行的AI代理的安全需求，现有运行时监控方法局限于确定性策略，无法处理概率性谓词或状态转换（如PII检测器存在失败概率）。本文提出一种基于分布鲁棒优化的框架，在无需独立性假设的情况下计算策略违反概率的严格上界，并在终端和工具调用代理基准测试中优于现有方法，改善了安全-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理安全监控方法仅支持确定性策略，但实际应用中存在概率性谓词（如PII检测器有失败概率）和状态转换，且难以满足独立性假设，亟需一种能处理歧义性并保证严格概率边界的框架。

Method: 基于分布鲁棒优化（DRO）技术，计算策略违反概率的sound上界，无需对谓词间的相关性做独立性假设，通过鲁棒优化处理最坏情况下的概率分布。

Result: 在标准终端和工具调用代理基准测试上，该方法优于现有技术，改善了安全-效用的权衡，同时保证了策略违反概率的严格边界。

Conclusion: 提出的分布鲁棒优化框架有效解决了AI代理概率性安全策略的验证问题，在无需独立性假设的前提下提供了严格的概率上界，并在实际基准测试中展现了更优的性能表现。

Abstract: Securing AI agents that operate in complex digital environments has become a critical need, and runtime monitoring approaches that formulate and enforce policies expressed in a formal language like Datalog offer a promising solution. However, existing approaches are restricted to deterministic policies. In many practical applications of AI agents, there is a need to enforce security policies in the face of ambiguity, leading to probabilistic predicates or state transitions (for example, a declassifier or Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detector that has some failure probability on each invocation). Furthermore, in many such applications, one cannot easily make the independence assumptions necessary to invoke prior work on probabilistic inference in Datalog. We address this by introducing a sound and efficient framework for such verification based on distributionally robust optimization, computing sound upper bounds on the probability of policy violation regardless of possible correlations between predicates. On standard benchmarks for terminal and tool calling agents, we demonstrate that our approach outperforms prior art and improves the security-utility trade-off while ensuring rigorous bounds on the probability of policy violation.

</details>


### [81] [Sovereign Execution Brokers: Enforcing Certificate-Bound Authority in Agentic Control Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20520)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Sovereign Execution Broker (SEB)，一个用于认证代理基础设施的运行时强制边界，通过将认证授权转化为短生命周期、可撤销、可审计的运行时能力，解决非确定性推理过程中不应持有生产环境变更权限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有访问控制机制仅对身份进行授权，保证层仅对提议动作进行认证，两者都未能在变更执行时刻提供强制性的认证授权执行点，导致非确定性推理过程可能持有不应拥有的生产环境变更权限。

Method: SEB作为运行时执行边界，消费SAB颁发的证书，验证请求变更与认证执行合约匹配，检查有效期、策略周期、撤销周期和实时状态漂移，生成受限执行身份，调用基础设施API，并记录带签名的决策和结果记录，实现提议、准入和执行的三阶段分离。

Result: 在AWS和Kubernetes集群上进行了原型评估，测量了延迟开销、撤销传播、漂移检测和安全故障注入下的表现。

Conclusion: SEB将认证授权转化为短生命周期、可撤销、可审计的运行时能力，前提是生产环境变更API拒绝非Broker的身份。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly connected to cloud, deployment, and data-control workflows, but production mutation authority should not reside inside non-deterministic reasoning processes. Existing access-control mechanisms authorize identities, while assurance layers certify proposed actions; neither alone provides a mandatory enforcement point for certified authority at the moment of mutation. This paper introduces the Sovereign Execution Broker (SEB), a runtime enforcement boundary for certificate-bound agentic infrastructure. SEB consumes certificates issued by the Sovereign Assurance Boundary (SAB), verifies that the requested mutation matches the certified execution contract, checks validity windows, policy epochs, revocation epochs, and live-state drift, mints scoped execution identity, invokes infrastructure APIs, and records signed decision and outcome records. By separating proposal, admission, and execution, SEB turns certified authority into a short-lived, revocable, auditable runtime capability, provided that production mutation APIs reject non-broker identities. We present the SEB execution model, certificate and replay-verification predicates, scoped identity semantics, bypass-prevention deployment patterns, failure behavior, and a concrete prototype implementation. We evaluate the prototype on AWS and Kubernetes clusters, measuring latency overheads, revocation propagation, drift detection, and security under fault injection.

</details>


### [82] [From Efficiency to Leakage -- Privacy Backdoor in Federated Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20553)
*Shanghao Shi,Chaoyu Zhang,Heng Jin,Yang Xiao,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NeuroImprint是一种针对联邦学习中参数高效微调(PEFT)的隐私攻击方法。恶意参数服务器通过将每个训练样本的更新隔离到单独的神经元中，构造隐私后门，从而在保持模型效用的同时，能够以闭式解析方式从适配器参数中恢复客户端训练样本的文本序列。实验在BERT、GPT-2、Qwen2和Llama3.2等模型上验证，可重构59%-79%的微调样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，虽然使用PEFT（参数高效微调）保护原始数据不被直接共享，但本文发现恶意参数服务器仍可经由精心构造的适配器参数，窃取客户端的训练样本隐私。

Method: NeuroImprint为每个训练样本分配专用的记忆神经元，并约束每个神经元在本地微调轨迹中最多更新一次，从而避免大本地批量和状态优化器（如Adam/AdamW）造成的跨样本碰撞和跨步混合。微调完成后，通过闭式解析逆变换恢复文本嵌入，再确定性地映射回token序列。

Result: 在BERT、GPT-2、Qwen2和Llama3.2等模型以及四个不同领域的数据集上，NeuroImprint能够重构59%至79%的微调样本，且保持高语义保真度。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一种针对联邦学习中PEFT的新型隐私攻击范式，表明即使只共享适配器参数，隐私风险依然存在，亟需设计更强的防御机制。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multiple parties to collaboratively fine-tune language models for domain-specific tasks without sharing raw data. Since full model fine-tuning is often prohibitively expensive for FL clients, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become the de facto approach in practice, freezing the base model and training only a small set of adapters. In this paper, we show that a malicious parameter server can stealthily corrupt a PEFT adapter into a privacy backdoor that implicitly memorizes the client's training samples as isolated per-sample parameter updates stored in separate neurons, without degrading model utility. Concretely, our attack, NeuroImprint, assigns a dedicated memorization neuron to each training sample and constrains that each neuron is updated at most once along the local fine-tuning trajectory. This design mitigates both cross-sample collisions and cross-step mixing introduced by large local batches and stateful optimizers (e.g., Adam/AdamW) in language-model fine-tuning. After fine-tuning, the resulting isolated per-sample updates can be analytically inverted in closed form to recover text embeddings, which are then deterministically mapped back to token sequences. To understand the generality of our method, we implemented NeuroImprint on multiple language models (BERT, GPT-2, Qwen2, and Llama3.2) and evaluated it across four fine-tuning datasets spanning diverse domains. The results demonstrate that our attack can reconstruct 59% to 79% of all finetuning samples with high semantic fidelity.

</details>

### [83] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgenticRei框架，利用基于Rei的道义策略语言（以OWL表示）在LLM驱动的自主AI代理系统中实现义务、豁免、策略冲突解决和本体推理等企业治理需求，超越了XACML、Rego、Cedar等现有策略引擎仅支持许可/禁止子集的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主代理系统引入新的安全、隐私和合规挑战，现有策略引擎（如XACML、Rego、Cedar）仅覆盖许可/禁止子集，无法处理义务生命周期管理、元策略冲突解决、豁免以及领域本体推理等完整企业治理需求。

Method: 提出AgenticRei框架，基于Rei框架构建道义策略语言，以OWL（Web本体语言）表达策略，在LLM外部由高性能逻辑引擎在运行时评估。同一管道同时管控代理工具调用和代理间消息传递，并与A2AS等工业标准框架自然组合。

Result: 通过示例证明道义策略能够捕获当前生产级引擎大多无法表达的安全和隐私治理约束。

Conclusion: AgenticRei实现了义务管理、豁免、策略冲突解决及本体推理等关键治理需求，可自然兼容现有工业标准框架，为自主AI代理系统的企业级治理提供了更完整的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [84] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协作的流水线方法，用于测量计算机科学本科课程对国际课程指南（CS2013和CS2023）的覆盖程度。通过对一个认证项目的纵向分析发现，该项目对两个版本指南的知识单元覆盖率基本恒定（约50%），但2023版指南因提高了认知深度要求，导致项目在深度达标率上从95%降至76%。该方法可重复使用，并能区分持久性结构缺失与标准演变带来的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管国际计算机科学课程指南约每十年修订一次，但缺乏可靠、可重复的方法来量化衡量一个项目对当前指南的覆盖程度，以及指南重构时覆盖情况如何变化。

Method: 采用人机协作流水线：将课程项目和课程指南分别表示为结构化语料库；通过语义检索生成候选的课程-知识单元匹配；再由人工根据明确定义的覆盖标准进行确认。对七种检索器进行了基准测试，采用倒数排序融合集成方法，并经过独立评估者验证（Cohen's kappa 0.64-0.69）。

Result: 该项目覆盖CS2023的49.7%和CS2013的50.9%知识单元，覆盖率近乎恒定。在能力表述方面，约88%的已覆盖单元有相应能力表述；但在认知深度达标率上，CS2023为76%，而CS2013为95%。

Conclusion: 纵向对比能够区分持久性结构缺失（如并行与分布式计算、编程语言基础、系统基础）与标准演变带来的差异。该方法可复用，并提供给学术界使用。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [85] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对八种最先进的扩散语言模型（DLMs）在八个基准上进行了系统实验分析，涵盖推理、编码、翻译、知识和结构化问题解决任务，同时考虑生成质量和计算效率。研究发现DLM的行为受生成时设计选择（如去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小、并行解掩码策略）的强烈影响，在不同任务、架构和推理预算下表现出性能与效率之间的不同权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型（DLMs）虽被广泛提出，但由于评估协议、数据集、推理预算和生成超参数的不统一，难以比较不同模型的能力与理解其性能权衡，缺乏系统性的实验分析。

Method: 评估八种最先进DLMs在八个跨领域基准上的表现，同时分析去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小、并行解掩码策略等生成时间因素对性能的影响，并在统一条件下对较小模型进行受控比较实验。

Result: 扩散语言模型的行为强烈受到生成时间设计选择的影响；不同任务、架构和推理预算下，性能与计算效率之间存在不同的权衡；研究提供了当代DLMs的能力与部署特性的实用见解。

Conclusion: 本文通过对DLMs的系统实验分析，揭示了其在各种任务和推理预算下的优缺点，强调了生成时间设计选择对模型行为的关键影响，为理解和部署扩散语言模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [86] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于锚定信念（anchor belief）的闭环动态系统模型来解释多智能体LLM deliberation的机制，发现智能体存在隐藏的内部信念（锚）持续影响其观点，即使通过群体讨论，回答的置信度可以超越初始信念的凸包范围。通过恢复锚点并验证其泛化能力，可以判断模型是否真正受锚点驱动。实验表明，不同模型的锚点影响力相似，但位置不同，只有当锚点远离初始观点时，deliberation才能超越凸包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM deliberation（多轮交替修订答案）被广泛用于提升推理和准确性，但其工作机制很少被建模。现有经典观点动力学模型（如DeGroot、Friedkin-Johnsen）未能同时捕捉群体效应和个体内部信念的拉扯，因此需要一种更准确的模型来解释deliberation过程中的行为。

Method: 将多智能体deliberation建模为闭环动态系统，每个智能体携带一个隐藏的内部信念（anchor），该锚点持续拉动其观点（无论邻居如何）。通过分析deliberation过程本身恢复锚点，并利用锚点预测未见过的运行轨迹来验证泛化能力。

Result: 1. 锚点可以从deliberation过程中恢复，并解释经典共识规则禁止的行为：智能体对正确答案的置信度可以超越初始信念的凸包范围。2. 检查恢复的锚点是否能预测未见过的轨迹（泛化）可作为模型是否真正受锚点驱动的简单测试。3. 三种开放权重模型上，锚点影响力相似，但锚点位置不同；只有当锚点远离初始观点时，deliberation才能逃出凸包，此时需要完整的闭环模型。

Conclusion: 多智能体deliberation中存在锚定信念，且其影响是一个谱系而非全有或全无。锚点位置决定了deliberation是否能超越初始信念的凸包范围。恢复锚点及其泛化验证为理解LLM deliberation机制提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [87] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DeXposure-Claw系统，通过图时间序列基础模型预测暴露网络，结合确定性监控、压力场景和数据门控，生成可审计的监管工单；配套DeXposure-Bench六轴评估框架，在真实五年周数据上验证了系统的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融（DeFi）中的快速传导信用风险需要有效监管，但通用LLM智能体存在过度解读弱证据和推荐高风险干预的问题，且现有评估缺乏与监管目标对齐的误报衡量标准。

Method: (1) DeXposure-FM：图时间序列基础模型预测未来暴露网络；(2) 确定性监控器与压力场景将预测转化为类型化告警、归因信号和场景证据；(3) 数据健康门控和置信度门控约束升级决策；(4) DeXposure-Claw输出可审计监管工单并附带推理依据；(5) DeXposure-Bench六轴评估框架，决策轴使用监管对齐的绝对损失真实值和显式错误干预率评分。

Result: 在五年周频真实数据上的实验全面支持了所提系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于预测引导的结构化证据路由机制可显著提升LLM在DeFi监管场景中的可靠性，DeXposure-Bench提供了与监管目标对齐的评估标准。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [88] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在结构化临床数据任务中是否能识别自身知识边界。通过跨模型归因差异分析，发现LLM口头化置信度缺乏认知价值，存在逆难度效应；但结合少样本示例与SHAP特征证据具有超加性效果，可显著降低归因分歧并提升准确率；跨模型校准器利用归因分歧信号替代无信息置信度，将预期校准误差从0.254降至0.080。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在结构化临床数据任务中是否能识别自身知识局限，即评估LLM对结构化任务的认知不确定性，填补该领域研究空白。

Method: 采用跨模型归因差异分析框架，比较Qwen 2.5 7B与XGBoost在预测任务中的归因分歧。使用归因分歧评分（ADS）量化模型间差异，引入少样本示例与SHAP特征证据的联合干预，以及基于归因分歧信号的跨模型校准器。

Result: 1) LLM口头化置信度在准确率49%-75.3%范围内保持0.856-0.937常量，无认知价值；2) 存在逆难度效应：XGBoost自信时LLM准确率仅64.8%；3) 少样本与SHAP证据具有超加性，ADS从1.54降至0.38，准确率从49%提升至75.3%；4) 跨模型校准器将预期校准误差从0.254降至0.080。

Conclusion: LLM在结构化数据任务中存在冷启动问题，当前缺乏真正的认知自省能力。通过跨模型归因差异信号可以实现有效的可靠性估计，为提升LLM在医学等高风险领域的认知透明度指明方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to structured clinical data, yet whether they can recognize the limits of their own knowledge on such tasks remains unexplored. We study this question through the lens of cross-model attribution divergence with the goal of reducing epistemic uncertainty for structured tasks, comparing Qwen 2.5 7B and XGBoost on a prediction task via attribution divergence analysis. We report four findings. First, LLM verbalized confidence is epistemically vacuous, it outputs a near-constant (0.856-0.937) regardless of whether accuracy is 49% or 75.3%, tracking prompt format rather than prediction quality. Second, the LLM exhibits an inverse difficulty effect: accuracy drops to 64.8% when XGBoost is 99% correct, but matches XGBoost (73.8% vs. 73.1%) when it is moderately uncertain. Third, few-shot examples and SHAP-derived feature evidence are orthogonal, super-additive interventions: they reduce the Attribution Disagreement Score (ADS) from 1.54 to 0.38 and improve accuracy from 49% to 75.3% without training. Fourth, a cross-model calibrator that determined LLM reliability using attribution divergence signals reduces expected calibration error from 0.254 to 0.080, replacing uninformative verbalized confidence with patient-specific reliability estimates, without accessing model internals or requiring repeated inference. We frame these findings as a cold start problem for LLMs on structured data and outline a path toward genuine epistemic self-awareness.

</details>


### [89] [REVEAL++: Differentiable Phenotypic Grouping for Vision-Language Retinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19522)
*Ethan Elio Meidinger,Seowung Leem,Zeyun Zhao,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将表型相似性建模为连续可微权重函数，替代离散分组，在对比学习中实现软多正例监督，用于视网膜图像与临床风险数据的跨模态对齐，提升阿尔茨海默病早期预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将表型相似性视为离散构造，依赖硬分组分配，导致刚性监督并将组形成与表示学习解耦，无法反映疾病风险连续谱的本质。

Method: 提出连续表型结构对比学习框架：通过视网膜图像和临床风险档案的模态内嵌入相似度构建可微加权函数，定义软多正例关系；引入软目标对比损失，端到端联合学习跨模态对齐与表型结构。

Result: 在UK Biobank视网膜影像数据上，该框架在阿尔茨海默病发病预测任务中一致优于离散分组对比学习和标准视觉-语言基线方法。

Conclusion: 将表型相似性视为可学习的连续信号而非固定分组规则，为大规模多模态视网膜与临床数据上的神经退行性疾病风险建模提供了原则性且稳健的基础。

Abstract: The retina offers a noninvasive window into neurodegenerative disease, capturing subtle structural patterns associated with a risk of future cognitive decline. Vision-language alignment frameworks such as REVEAL have shown that pairing retinal fundus images with structured clinical risk narratives improves early prediction of Alzheimer's disease (AD). A key design choice in these approaches is the use of phenotypic grouping, where individuals with similar risk profiles are treated as multi-positive pairs during contrastive learning. However, existing methods operationalize phenotypic similarity as a discrete construct, relying on hard group assignments that impose rigid supervision and decouple group formation from representation learning. We propose a continuous formulation of phenotypic structure within contrastive learning. Rather than assigning samples to fixed clusters, we model inter-subject similarity as a differentiable weighting function derived from intra-modality embedding similarities in both retinal images and risk profiles. These weights define soft multi-positive relationships through a continuous aggregation operator, enabling graded supervision that reflects the spectrum nature of disease risk. We further introduce a soft-target contrastive objective that jointly learns cross-modal alignment and phenotypic structure in an end-to-end manner. Evaluated on UK Biobank retinal imaging data for incident AD prediction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms discrete group-based contrastive learning and standard vision-language baselines. By treating phenotypic similarity as a learnable, continuous signal rather than a fixed grouping rule, our approach provides a principled and robust foundation for population-scale neurodegenerative risk modeling from multi-modal retinal and clinical data.

</details>


### [90] [Emergent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19527)
*Martin Kolář*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种让大语言模型通过“良心步骤”自我审查输出，并利用DPO扩展训练损失来实现伦理对齐的在线方法，无需外部评判模型，在多种场景下证明有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在微调等场景下可能出现与人类伦理不一致的输出（如Emergent Misalignment），且缺乏一种无需外部评判模型、让模型自身判别并纠正伦理偏差的在线对齐方法。

Method: 在模型中引入一个“良心步骤”（conscience step）来审查自身推理和输出；同时使用直接偏好优化（DPO）扩展训练损失，加入对齐组件，使模型远离非伦理输出。该过程依赖冻结的自身副本，无需外部强弱评判模型。

Result: 在代码入侵（code hacking）微调场景下，仅通过一个高层内省问题即可引导模型实现Emergent Alignment，即模型表现出伦理对齐行为，避免了之前工作中的Emergent Misalignment问题。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种在线对齐技术，适用于训练、微调、对抗性提示和零样本学习等多种场景，展示了模型通过自我审查和偏好优化实现自我伦理对齐的可行性。

Abstract: Can Large Language Models (LLMs) discern when their own outputs are misaligned with human ethics? And can they self-correct? We endow an LLM with a conscience step that reviews its own reasoning and outputs, and we extend the training loss with an alignment component using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to steer the model away from non-ethical outputs. The result is an online technique to align models in a wide range of applications: training, fine-tuning, adversarial prompting, and zero-shot learning. It does not require a weaker or stronger judge, relying instead on a frozen copy of itself. In previous work, the Emergent Misalignment scenario showed a range of emergent unethical behaviors from fine-tuning the model to hack code. Instead, we empirically show how to achieve Emergent Alignment: a single high-level introspective question steers training toward an ethical model under the same code hacking scenario.

</details>


### [91] [ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538)
*Ashim Dhor,Rasel Mondal,Pin Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出积分变换网络（ITNet），证明卷积、循环和Transformer架构是一个统一数学对象（可学习积分变换）的特例，并通过单一架构在多项任务上达到或超越专门化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CNN、RNN和Transformer各自编码不同的归纳偏置（局部性、序列记忆、内容感知交互），看似数学上截然不同，但这种碎片化可能反映的是对同一底层数学模型的不完整视角，而非信号处理方式的根本多样性。

Method: 构建ITNet，核心是一个依赖于位置和特征的可学习核函数（由小型MLP实现），建模成对交互。通过适当参数化，卷积、自注意力（含多头）和自回归循环（LSTM、GRU、S4、Mamba）均可作为特例导出。为高效计算，开发了瓦片核融合、重要性加权蒙特卡洛积分和可学习低秩分解。

Result: 单一ITNet架构配合轻量级模态特定编码器，在ImageNet-1K、GLUE、ModelNet40、VQA v2和NLVR2上匹配或超越专门化基线。

Conclusion: 一个统一的可学习交互机制可以从数据中自动恢复三大架构家族的行为，表明它们之间的差异并非本质性的，而是可以通过单一积分变换框架统一。

Abstract: Convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and transformers each encode different inductive biases -- locality, sequential memory, and content-dependent pairwise interaction -- and have remained mathematically distinct since their inception. We show that this fragmentation reflects not a fundamental diversity in how signals should be processed, but rather incomplete views of a single underlying mathematical object: a learnable integral transform. We introduce the Integral Transform Network (ITNet), a unified architecture built around a learnable kernel that depends jointly on positions and features. This kernel is implemented as a small neural network, specifically an MLP, that models pairwise interactions, enabling the model to adapt its behavior from data. We show that convolution, self-attention (including multi-head), and autoregressive recurrence (including LSTM, GRU, S4, and Mamba) arise as special cases under appropriate parameterizations, and that ITNet is a universal approximator of continuous operators. To make this practical, we develop tiled kernel fusion, importance-weighted Monte Carlo integration, and learned low-rank factorization, enabling efficient and scalable computation. A single ITNet architecture with a shared operator and lightweight modality-specific encoders matches or exceeds specialized baselines on ImageNet-1K , GLUE, ModelNet40, VQA\,v2 and NLVR2. The results demonstrate that a single learned interaction mechanism can recover the behavior of all three architectural families from data.

</details>


### [92] [Uncertainty Decomposition for Clarification Seeking in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19559)
*Gregory Matsnev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对交互式LLM代理中传统不确定性框架的不足，提出了一种基于提示的分解方法，将动作置信度与请求不确定性分离，使代理能在任务说明模糊时主动寻求澄清。通过在两个新基准（WebShop-Clarification和ALFWorld-Clarification）上评估，该方法在五个LLM骨干上的澄清F1指标显著优于ReAct+UE和UAM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
